Therefore **both reported default file/directory paths are absent** on the owner's machine. This does not prove every potential OpenSSL trust mechanism is absent or predict the exact result of a real certificate verification. Original earlier relative `DllImport` probe failed with null path (not a certificate diagnostic); the corrected absolute-load probe succeeded. ### Production deployment sequencing — STANDING RELEASE GATE / DOCS ONLY (2026-10-23)

- **Rule: data collection has priority over site deployment.** For any future deployment that will advance `site-prod` (even a frontend-only change), first inspect the live GitHub Actions states of `Update Ukraine air-alert production data` (`main:.github/workflows/update-netlify-site.yml`) and any other active writer of `site-prod`, plus its next expected cron window. If an updater is `queued`, `waiting`, or `in_progress`, **put the deployment in the release queue and do not push to `site-prod`**. Also defer when a scheduled update is due, overdue or imminent enough to overlap with the planned deployment; GitHub cron starts may be delayed. Clock time alone or the absence of a visible run does not prove that a scheduled slot was safely skipped.
- **Release only after the data write has ended.** Confirm the updater's terminal **SUCCESS**, successful production data/checkpoint publication, and current `site-prod` HEAD. Rebase/rebuild the already approved release onto that **new** HEAD, preserve the newly published source/checkpoint/dashboard data, recheck the exact diff and required regressions, and check updater status again **immediately before** the guarded `site-prod` update. Use an expected-HEAD/fast-forward guard; never force-push or overwrite newer data. Netlify publication must follow the guarded Git promotion.
- **Failure / ambiguous state = hold, not bypass.** If the updater fails, is delayed with uncertain status, or the HEAD changes during release preparation, keep the release queued and investigate/verify the latest authoritative data state. Do not cancel the data updater, dispatch blind retries, silently skip its checkpoint, or treat a successful frontend deployment as proof of data freshness. Resume only when the conflict has been resolved and the release gate independently passes; escalate if necessary.
- **Incident motivating the rule:** scheduled [updater run `39960968742`](https://github.com/olegbalakin-cmyk/kyiv-air-alerts-grafana/actions/runs/39960968742) (2026-10-23) checked out old `site-prod@fd1c2aa1`; the approved frontend/data deployment moved the branch to `95165340` while collection was running. At `13:50 UTC`, the updater's `Persist verified UkraineAlarm bridge checkpoint` push was rejected `non-fast-forward`; downstream data publication did not run. Last published generation remained `2026-10-23T07:47:16+03:00`. **Next action is observation of the next scheduled run, not a manual restart.**
- **Scope and enforcement:** This is an **operator/coordinator release policy**, recorded in `PROJECT_STATE.md` and `CHANGELOG.md`, **not an implemented GitHub Actions queue or lock**. Automating it requires separate code, isolated tests and authorization. No workflow, `site-prod`, Netlify, Neon/H2, historical/backfill, WIP, or casualty track is modified by this documentation transition.

### Production partial periods stale after overlapping frontend release — READ-ONLY INCIDENT CONFIRMED (2026-10-20)

- **Observation / source evidence:** As of the 2026-10-19T21:41Z independent readback (already 2026-10-20 in Europe/Kyiv), `site-prod` HEAD remained `4c8dafab15d26f0220eb911c67844d522974081d`; last successful production data commit `fc88b3ff1fea4b9cab3405e29b626355dd9a3828` at `2026-10-19T16:02:35Z`, with `data/dashboard_qa.json:generated_at=2026-10-19T16:01:10.782024Z`. Committed Kyiv `short_28d.csv` ends **2026-10-18**, `weekly.csv` ends complete calendar week **2026-10-12..18**, and regular `monthly.csv` through **2026-09**. Last successful production updater [run 39279105934](https://github.com/olegbalakin-cmyk/kyiv-air-alerts-grafana/actions/runs/39279105934). Large `dashboard_data.json` / `site_derived.json` raw JSON were not independently loaded; their Git blob IDs and generating scripts were inspected instead.
- **Root cause confirmed in original GitHub job log:** [production updater run 39324278722](https://github.com/olegbalakin-cmyk/kyiv-air-alerts-grafana/actions/runs/39324278722), job `118305956500`, started **2026-10-19T21:02Z** from base `fc88b3ff`. User-authorized frontend production release advanced `site-prod` to `4c8dafab` at **21:14Z while updater was still running**. At **21:33:26Z**, step `Persist verified UkraineAlarm bridge checkpoint` attempted `git push origin HEAD:site-prod` after validation (`required_rows=21`, `continuity_verified_rows=21`, `fully_continuous=true`) but received exact **`! [rejected] HEAD -> site-prod (non-fast-forward)`** and deliberately failed closed instead of replacing newer HEAD. All subsequent derived/partial-period build, QA and production-data commit steps **SKIPPED**. This is a concurrency collision triggered by frontend branch advancement, not a demonstrated data-source or chart-calculation failure. No stored bridge checkpoint or new derived partial periods landed.
- **Period-specific analysis:** Existing `pages-preview/app.js` `mergeCurrentDayDaily28` admits the generated current-day overlay only when `meta.current_day` is exactly the current calendar date in `Europe/Kyiv`; the stale last-success payload was built on **Oct 19**, so on **Oct 20** the guard rejects yesterday's overlay, and the base daily series ends **Oct 18**. `derivedCalendarWeeks` requires the current week's Monday in `daily28`; with last complete day **Sun Oct 18**, it cannot add partial calendar week **Oct 19..25**. Production `apply_site_rolling_7d.py:add_partial_month` is designed to append a flagged partial October entry through the last completed day (**Oct 18** when last run succeeded); October is therefore **stale**, not confirmed entirely absent, because direct raw multi-megabyte JSON/browser rendering was not independently inspected. Separate cumulative-hours comparison and custom all-city monthly aggregate *intentionally exclude* partial months. Production frontend release diff changes header/logo/share/embed and deep-link handling only, not partial-series generation or filtering.
- **Status/action boundary:** `PRODUCTION_PARTIAL_PERIOD_FRESHNESS=DEGRADED — CONFIRMED`; `UPDATER_SITE_PROD_NON_FAST_FORWARD_COLLISION=CONFIRMED`; **RECOVERY=NOT EXECUTED**. This audit was read-only for production/WIP/Neon/casualty/historical/backfill. **Next bounded action, separately authorized:** ensure no competing `site-prod` writer, run a fresh production data updater (not a blind historical rerun) and verify successful Git data commit, freshness and current day/calendar week/partial October across source CSV, JSON and browser. Separately harden safe bounded retry/rebase for frontend-only branch movement while preserving checkpoint monotonicity, bridge verification, compare-and-swap and never force-pushing or dropping newer data. No user-facing claim of a fix until independently verified successful run/readback.

Post-final-toggle authoritative workflow registry `state` **NOT INDEPENDENTLY READ** (GitHub connector denies GET workflow endpoints with its URL allowlist; not an actual GitHub 403); observed later sandbox run list remains 10 runs, no new attempts. Treat final-off status as **USER-REPORTED / READBACK PENDING**. - **Important independent static-review finding, not covered by T1–T14:** In frozen writer `persist_episode_classification()`, `commit_state` is computed via `conn.info.transaction_status == INTRANS` and **returned from inside** `with conn.transaction():`. The status is sampled before the context exits/commits; thus even a normal successful owned/autocommit=True writer transaction is tagged `CALLER_TRANSACTION_PENDING`. `persist_due_episode_classifications()` requires `COMMITTED` and raises `PERSISTENCE_ACK_BEFORE_COMMIT`, potentially **after a row has committed**. This consequence follows from exact code order and must be reproduced/fixed in a dedicated positive-path batch test; the current test suite only calls the batch API on rejection paths (T14). Direct writer insert/CAS/rollback assertions remain useful, but **durable batch ACK / end-to-end application success = NOT PROVEN; defect suspected with high confidence from code inspection**.
- **Next narrow execution task:** Repair commit-state computation **after** exiting the writer's transaction context; preserve caller-owned outer-transaction pending semantics. Add real PG18 positive batch tests with `expected_tips`, durable independent readback, exact retry, injected error, no premature/false ACK, and a regression negative control; independently verify the fixed frozen writer. Separately establish operational caller's authoritative `expected_current_tip` origin and canonical PostgreSQL schema equivalence **before** any separately authorized disposable Neon child test. No Neon branch/SQL changes or production, site-prod, operational WIP, classifier, casualty, backfill or cutover promotion. ## 1. Project purpose

The original Kyiv Grafana air-alert dashboard has grown into a repository with distinct tracks: public air-alert collection and presentation; alert-to-attack classification and live multicity processing; historical attack-event reconstruction and a separate Kyiv historical-discovery development effort; human-reviewed civilian-casualty discovery and promotion; and bounded canonical persistence/Neon work. The original dashboard documentation is [`kyiv-air-alerts-grafana/README.md`](kyiv-air-alerts-grafana/README.md). It remains the dashboard manual, not the cross-track project status.

### Independent 23-city casualty coverage/follow-up audit design — READY / ACCEPTED (methodology only)

- **Independent acceptance:** `CASUALTY 23-CITY COVERAGE AND FOLLOW-UP AUDIT DESIGN = READY — ACCEPTED`; `CASUALTY 23-CITY AUDIT DESIGN INDEPENDENT ACCEPTANCE = PASS`. Frozen **external** ZIP `casualty_23city_audit_design_2026-10-11.zip` (**29,910 bytes**, SHA-256 `dbfd335c406153f276345d08108e089917adeddffd8eb674b315becc20e9ad88`), not committed to main: **6/6 CRC**, **5/5 internal SHA-256**, Draft 2020-12 JSON Schema valid (**13 row kinds**), A/B isolated `BUILD_CONTRACT.py` reconstructions byte-identical with frozen schema and sample plan. This is methodology acceptance only, **not** an executed pilot/audit or any measurement of recall, precision, timeliness or W7 performance.
- **Operational/population definition:** 23 source-code-confirmed city keys (**4 exact-city, 19 district-proxy alert triggers**); civilian-death attribution limited to legal **city** boundaries, not district/oblast trigger geography. Independent attack frame includes zero-initial-death and no-completed-alert waves; eligible aerial attacks separate from extended non-aerial deaths. WIP `when:8d` Google News RSS, completed-alert trigger, +0/+24/+72/+168h **alert-end** polling, candidate URL/title dedup, separate Kyiv body-hash source queue and strictly manual review/confirm/publish path are not interchangeable with independent truth.
- **Seven separate metrics and observable stages:** (1) **city-wave attack** discovery, (2) **fatal-city-wave casualty evidence** discovery, (3) independently supported **death units**, omissions/duplicates (count agreement only where no person-to-ledger linkage), (4) **WIP monitor-candidate** PPV, (5) **source/fact** detection latency, (6) **wave/site/causal-alert** matching correctness and coverage, (7) **substantive W7 fatality update facts**. Reference, raw `SOURCE_DISCOVERY`, WIP candidate, human review, confirmed revision and published aggregate each need separate identity/timing proof; an alert is not an attack, and monthly counts are not unique people.
- **Sampling and uncertainty:** prospective 12 complete UTC weeks = **276 city-week cells**, probability P1 base at most **46** city-waves (one per nonempty city/six-week half), up to **24** independently selected nonprobability diagnostic controls excluded from weighted population estimates absent a known union inclusion model, and up to **40** probability-sampled monitor candidates; at most **110** pilot units before overlap. Freeze `N_h`, `pi_i`, PRNG/seed, source frame and blinded decisions. Planning effective n **385/97** (nominal ±5/10 pp at 95%), **577/145** with assumed DEFF 1.5; small pilot CANNOT provide precise 23-city population-wide recall. Stratum/cluster uncertainty, sparse zeros, unresolved bounds and incomplete external source ascertainment must remain explicit.
- **Follow-up contract:** `W7 = attack t0 +168h` (UTC), and extra **48h for monitor detection delay only**, never extending W7 reference facts. Distinguish new/hospital deaths, duplicate cumulative numbers, location corrections, retractions and delayed publications. Date-only times are bounded uncertain, not midnight. Preserve `ZP-1006` **wave-only** with specific warehouse UNSUPPORTED, `OD-0926` contradictory fatality site, `IF-1008` unresolved unique causal alert relationship; the accepted **25-event site/count/follow-up validation remains PARTIAL**.

These **2,456 / 145** historical reference totals are **not** the denominator in the new development sample. Development uses a frozen **67-episode** cohort: **48 known positives** and **19 holds**. A 411-episode continuity replay is a regression check; it is neither a new validation cohort nor a production backfill.

Multicity casualty candidate discovery and its human review queue are on `multicity-wip-2026-09-16`. Prior reports describe required human approval, single-operator self-review and disabled administrator bypass; the effective rollout flag and current account-side protection have not been independently reverified in this documentation checkpoint.

## 7. How another ChatGPT chat should use this file

1. Read `PROJECT_STATE.md` before proposing work, then `CHANGELOG.md` for accepted transitions.
2. Verify the exact referenced proof artifact, immutable identity and branch head for the **one track** being changed; this file is a pointer, not proof-by-itself.
3. Act on one bounded failure class and state permitted mutations and forbidden changes explicitly.
4. Separate **production**, **development**, **historical reference**, **backfill** and **human-reviewed casualty** evidence.
5. Update `PROJECT_STATE.md` only after an accepted, reproducible checkpoint.
6. Append to `CHANGELOG.md` only for an accepted state transition; omit routine workflow attempts and debugging retries.
